==========================================================================================
Triangle factors  \mathcal{E}^lambda_{sigma1 sigma2}
lambda, sigma1, sigma2 in {L,+,-}; 27 helicity combinations.
The Triangle branch label is d: x = -i d \tilde\nu.
==========================================================================================


======================================================================
lambda = L
======================================================================

>>> PLAIN FORM (no phi-average) <<<
\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{L}_{LL} =
\cos\phi**4*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**4/K**2 + \cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3/K**2 + 2*\cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3*\cos\theta/K**2 + \cos\phi**2*\sin\phi**2*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**4/K**2 + \cos\phi**2*\cos\theta_1**2*k_s*q*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/K**2 + \cos\phi**2*\cos\theta_1**2*q**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/K**2 + \cos\phi**2*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/K**2 + \cos\phi**2*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/K**2 + \cos\phi*\sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3/K**2 + 2*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3*\cos\theta/K**2 + \cos\phi*\cos\theta_1*k_s**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta/K**2 + 3*\cos\phi*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**2/K**2 + 2*\cos\phi*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**3/K**2 + \sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1**2*k_s*q*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/K**2 + \sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1**2*q**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/K**2 + \cos\theta_1**2*k_s**2*\cos\theta**2/K**2 + 2*\cos\theta_1**2*k_s*q*\cos\theta**3/K**2 + \cos\theta_1**2*q**2*\cos\theta**4/K**2

\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{L}_{L+} =
\cos\phi**4*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) + i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) + \cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) - \cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) + \cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**3/(2*K**2) + \cos\phi**2*\sin\phi**2*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) + i*\cos\phi**2*\sin\phi*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) + i*\cos\phi**2*\sin\phi*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) - \cos\phi**2*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) + i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**3*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) + \cos\phi*\sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) - \cos\phi*\sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) + \cos\phi*\sin\phi**2*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**3/(2*K**2) - i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2/(2*K**2) - i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) - \cos\phi*\cos\theta_1*k_s**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta/(2*K**2) + \cos\phi*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta**3/(2*K**2) - i*\sin\phi*\cos\theta_1*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta/K**2 - i*\sin\phi*\cos\theta_1*q**2*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) + \cos\theta_1**2*k_s**2*\sin\theta**2/(2*K**2) + \cos\theta_1**2*k_s*q*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta/K**2 + \cos\theta_1**2*q**2*\sin\theta**2*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2)

\overline{\mathcal{E}}^{L}_{++} =

i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**3*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta/4 - i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**3*k_s*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta**2/(4*K) + i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**3*k_s*\sin\theta_1**2/(4*K) - i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**3*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta**3/(4*K) + i*\cos\phi*\sin\phi**3*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta/(4*K) - \overline{\mathcal{E}}^{L}_{+-} =

i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*k_s*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta**2/(4*K) + i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*k_s*\sin\theta_1**2/(4*K) - i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta**3/(4*K) + i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta/(4*K) + \cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*k_s*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta/(4*K) + \cos\phi**3*\cos\theta_1*q*\sin\theta_1*\sin\theta*\cos\theta/(2*K) - i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*k_s**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta/(4*K**2) - i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*k_s*q*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta**2/(2*K**2) - i*\cos\phi**3*\sin\phi*q**2*\sin\theta_1**2*\cos\theta**3/(4*K**2) -
